## Corpus Check
- 599 files · ~3,466,948 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 55874 nodes · 104163 edges · 2345 communities (1713 shown, 632 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 94% EXTRACTED · 6% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5984 edges (avg confidence: 0.92)
- Token cost: 241,799 input · 12,601 output
